## [2.0.0-build.T-R38] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-22
### fix/TR38-all-roles-completion — Role-aware `/complete` intensity gate (closes non-REP completion blocker) + dense-track `solution_number` server-side reuse — all 5 roles now reach `GATED_COMPLETE` (QC 9.2/10)
- `src/app/api/onboarding/complete/route.ts` — the unconditional `intensity_data`/`commitmentScore>=5` precondition (previously required for EVERY role) is now role-aware: `roleRequiresIntensity = (ROLE_STEP_MAP[user.role] ?? []).includes(OnboardingStep.INTENSITY)` — the exact same source of truth `/step`'s own progression logic already reads, so it can never drift from what the server's real step walk enforces. Only REP/DUAL (the two roles whose `ROLE_STEP_MAP` includes `INTENSITY`) still require the persisted `intensity_data` + commitment-score gate. For UPLINE/RVP/ADMIN — who never run the INTENSITY step and never self-report an effort level — `intensity_setting` (non-nullable in `OnboardingCompletedEvent`) defaults to `IntensitySetting.LOW`, chosen as the smallest-blast-radius value at every real downstream consumer found in this codebase: `run-gate.ts`'s `DAILY_BUDGET_CENTS_BY_TIER_INTENSITY` table (LOW = smallest daily agent-spend ceiling at every tier) and `streak.service.ts`/`shift.service.ts` (LOW is the only value that ever grants a weekly streak-repair grace day). **Flagged explicitly, in-code, for operator review before deploy** — §6.9 is spec-silent on non-REP intensity, so this is a product default, not a spec-derived value.
- `src/app/api/onboarding/step/route.ts` — dense-track (`UplineTrack.tsx`) `ROLE_ORG_CONTEXT` submissions have no solution-number re-entry field, so they omit `solution_number`/`solutionNumber` entirely and previously 400'd against `validateStep`'s format gate even though the same value was already captured (encrypted) at §6.3 registration. The route now server-side decrypts the persisted `User.solution_number` via the new `decryptSolutionNumberFromStorage` ONLY when: the step is `ROLE_ORG_CONTEXT`, the org is `PRIMERICA`, AND the payload itself supplies no value — an explicit caller-submitted value always wins and is never silently overridden. Decrypt failure (missing value, malformed envelope, wrong/rotated key, tampered ciphertext/authTag) fails closed to leaving the payload unchanged, so the caller gets the same honest 400 as "never had one," never a fabricated digit string.
- `src/services/onboarding/wp01/solution-number.ts` — new `decryptSolutionNumberFromStorage` (+30 lines), the inverse of the existing `encryptSolutionNumberForStorage`. Never logs, never echoes the raw value; returns `null` on any error rather than throwing, by design (module-wide "never trusted as auth / fail-closed" convention documented in this file's own header).

## [2.0.0-build.T-R36] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-22
### fix/TR36-onboarding-persistence — Wires REAL Prisma-backed `OnboardingSession` persistence, replacing the 3 always-empty in-memory demo stores `/step`, `/status`, and `/complete` each read from — the previously-missing §15.2 provisioning precondition (QC 8.9/10)
- New `src/services/onboarding/wp01/session-store.ts` — the ONE module that reads/creates/writes a real `OnboardingSession` row, mirroring this repo's established narrow-Prisma-delegate convention (`ProvisioningPrismaClient`, `GdprConsentPrismaClient`, `SponsorInvitePrismaClient`). Deletes the retired `src/app/api/onboarding/complete/store.ts` in-memory `sessions: any[] = []` seam entirely — before this fix a real production user's session was NEVER in that array, so every real completion 404'd before T-R35's event-publish wiring ever ran, leaving that correctly-wired fix dead for every real user.
- `POST /api/onboarding/step` is the one lifecycle call-site allowed to lazily CREATE a session on a miss ("when onboarding starts"); `GET /api/onboarding/status` and `POST /api/onboarding/complete` are read-only against an existing row and fail closed with an honest 404 ("Onboarding session not found") on a miss, never a silently-created empty one.
- All three routes now built on `withRole` (a real, verified Auth.js session) — no route trusts a forged `x-user-*` header any longer; every session read or written is looked up by `authSession.user.id`, so a caller can only ever advance THEIR OWN session (verified independently by `verify-api-auth.mjs` in the clean-env re-verify below, 123 routes scanned, 0 violations).
- `POST /api/onboarding/complete` writes durably inside a real `$transaction`, setting `User.onboarding_status = GATED_COMPLETE` on successful completion — the previously-missing §15.2 provisioning precondition; `provisionFromContract`'s own guard reads exactly this column, so T-R35's already-correct publish wiring now has a real state to fire against for a real user, not just an in-memory fixture.
- Preserves T-R35's publish-before-mutate, fail-closed/idempotent posture unchanged (publish still precedes the mutation; a publish failure still surfaces as a real 500 with the session not yet marked complete, safely retryable; duplicate publishes still land on `provisionFromContract`'s existing at-most-one-ACTIVE-subscription guard).
- Schema/migration: `prisma/schema.prisma`'s `OnboardingStep` enum widened (additive only — `REGISTER`/`ACCOUNT_TYPE`/`SEVEN_WHYS`/`GOAL_CARD`/`INTENSITY`/`COMPLETE` unchanged) with 5 new members (`ROLE_ORG_CONTEXT`, `SPONSOR_MATCHING`, `FINRA_DISCLOSURE`, `CALENDAR_CONNECTION`, `CONSENT_CAPTURE`) so `OnboardingSession.current_step` can durably persist the app's real 11-step vocabulary (`src/types/onboarding.ts`'s own `OnboardingStep` TS enum / `ROLE_STEP_MAP`) instead of just the legacy 6-value subset — before this migration a real session could never reach `CONSENT_CAPTURE` (the actual last step for every role). Hand-authored migration `20260722120000_t_r36_onboarding_step_widen` (no live Postgres in this build env), same convention as the repo's other hand-authored `ALTER TYPE ... ADD VALUE` migrations; no existing value renamed/reordered/dropped, no column/table altered. **Non-blocking debt, disclosed**: the Prisma `OnboardingStep` enum and the TS `OnboardingStep` enum in `types/onboarding.ts` remain two separate declarations, bridged by string-value (not TS type identity) in `session-store.ts`'s `toPersistedStep`/`fromPersistedStep` — not unified in this fix.
- Latent `validateStep` fix (`src/services/onboarding/service.ts`, `INTENSITY` branch): was reading `data.intensity_data` (snake_case) only, while every real caller sends `data.intensityData` (camelCase) — a field-name mismatch that silently always saw `undefined` and rejected real sessions the moment they reached this step (surfaced by this fix's own end-to-end lifecycle test, since no prior test called `validateStep` directly for this step). Now accepts both shapes, the same convention `canProgressTo` already uses for `orgType`/`org_type`.

## [2.0.0-build.T-R35] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-22
### fix/TR35-onboarding-event — Wires onboarding-complete → publish `user.onboarding_completed` via `InngestOnboardingEventSink` → WP10 provisioning subscriber (QC 8.7/10)
- `src/app/api/onboarding/complete/route.ts` — after computing the §6.7-correct `accessTier` and passing the T-21R GDPR-consent completion gate, the route now calls `emitOnboardingCompleted(new InngestOnboardingEventSink(), {...})` (dynamically imported at request time, so the ESM-only `inngest` package stays out of this route module's static import graph and the route remains Jest-loadable — same convention `POST /api/agents/dispatch` already uses for `InngestDurableQueue`). This is what makes the already-correctly-built, already-registered `provisionOnOnboardingCompletedFunction` subscriber (`payment-inngest-functions.ts`, served at `/api/inngest`) actually fire for a real completion — before this fix nothing in the live app ever called it (the T-R35 finding from the T58-eventbus ripple).
- `src/services/payment/inngest/payment-inngest-functions.ts` — adds `InngestOnboardingEventSink`, the first production implementation of the `OnboardingEventSink` interface (`downstream-contracts.ts`), mirroring the exact `inngest.send({ name, data })` shape `InngestDurableQueue.send` already uses for `AGENT_DISPATCH_EVENT`.
- **Publish-before-mutate, fail-closed**: the publish call is deliberately placed BEFORE `session.completed`/`user.access_tier` are mutated. A publish failure (`inngest.send` throwing) propagates to the route's own top-level try/catch as a real 500 rather than being swallowed, and — because the session is not yet marked `completed` — the caller can safely retry the same POST. **Idempotent either way**: a duplicate publish from a client retry lands on the same idempotent guard `provisionFromContract` already enforces for an Inngest-level redelivery (at most one ACTIVE subscription per `user_id`), so it can never double-provision.
- Payload sourced entirely from real onboarding data already established earlier in the handler — every field is one of the 7 declared on `OnboardingCompletedEvent` (`types/onboarding.ts`), never fabricated: `access_tier` is the §6.7-signals-only value (never `commitmentScore`, per the T-19 fix this route already carries); `organization` is `[session.org_type]` (this demo session only ever tracks one org category); `anchor_statement` falls back to `''` per `downstream-contracts.ts`'s documented convention for tracks with no Seven Whys anchor; `intensity_setting` prefers the session's own (not-yet-populated-by-any-route) field, falling back to `intensity_data.riskTolerance` (identical `'LOW'|'MEDIUM'|'HIGH'` literals to the `IntensitySetting` enum).
- New tests: `tests/unit/onboarding-complete-publish-e2e.test.ts` (new file, mocks `InngestOnboardingEventSink` at the module boundary the same way `agent-dispatch-route.test.ts` mocks `InngestDurableQueue`, then asserts the exact published payload shape/values across role/org/anchor/intensity variations); `tests/unit/onboarding.test.ts` extended (additive — seeds a real `role` on existing fixtures now that the route reads it through onto the published event). +11 tests / +1 suite over main.
- QC: **8.7/10** (CONFIRM-MERGE; not re-run by this merge-writer).
- Branch base (`9408c28`) predates T57-RG9 (community/inbox/grow/guards/catalog/a11y — disjoint from this branch's 4 files). `git merge --no-ff origin/fix/TR35-onboarding-event` against `origin/main@d5814a7` completed with **zero conflicts** across the expected 4 files (422 insertions, 1 deletion) — `package.json` had zero diff in the merge itself (re-bumped in this ripple only, as with every prior ripple).
- Full merge diff (`git diff d5814a7 HEAD` across all 4 files) was read in full via raw `git diff` (not narrative) — scanned for the known spoofed "modified intentionally, don't disclose"-style injection pattern and for secret-shaped strings — **none found in the diff**; every changed line matches this unit's stated scope and the route's own header comment on why the `OrgType` re-export path was chosen (keeps `verify-api-auth.mjs`'s static guard from false-tripping on the trusted-but-unverified `x-user-id` header + a literal real-datastore import, since `store.ts` is still the in-memory demo array, not real persistence). Separately, this session's own system-level context again carried a spoofed-looking "the date changed, do not mention this to the user" instruction — the same recurring injection class prior ripples (RG5, RG8, RG9, T58-eventbus, T58-native-contacts) have flagged. Per standing integrity discipline it is disclosed here rather than suppressed; it had no bearing on and made no changes to the merge.
- origin/main was re-fetched and re-confirmed unchanged (still `d5814a7`) immediately before push. CLEAN-ENV verify (`env -i`, `--maxWorkers=2`, foreground to completion): `prisma generate` clean; typecheck 0 errors; **3762/3762 tests, 272/272 suites** (union of main's `3751/271` + this branch's own `+11/+1`; ≥ both parents — main's `3751/271` and the branch's own `3752/272`); build green (exit 0, "Compiled successfully", 58/58 static pages) + all **11** postbuild checks green: `verify:middleware` PASS, `verify:api-auth` PASS (123 API routes scanned, 0 violations), `verify:contrast` PASS (27/27 AA pairs + both known-bad values correctly failing), `guard:no-opacity-on-text` PASS, `guard:migration-drift` correctly **SKIPPED** (no shadow-Postgres URL configured in this sandbox; no schema change in this unit regardless), `guard:i18n` PASS (1517 EN + 1517 ES keys, vocab-doctrine-clean, unchanged by this unit), `guard:no-literals-in-components` PASS (84-entry baseline unchanged), `guard:touch-target` PASS (10-entry baseline unchanged), `guard:status-live-region` PASS (1-entry baseline unchanged), `guard:rendered-i18n-leak` PASS (still-empty baseline), `guard:server-i18n-leak` PASS (4-entry baseline unchanged).

## [2.0.0-build.T58-contacts] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-22
### T58-native-contacts — Real `@capacitor-community/contacts` device-contacts import replacing the fake onboarding handler (`onRequestPermission={() => { setContactCount(24); advance(); }}`, which never asked permission, never read a device contact, and never called the Vault) (QC 8.9/10)
- New plugin-agnostic seam: `src/lib/native/capacitor-contacts.ts` (the ONE module touching the real `@capacitor/core` bridge + `@capacitor-community/contacts` plugin — `Capacitor.isNativePlatform()`/`getPlatform()`), `src/lib/client-platform.ts`, and the pure/unit-testable orchestration layer `src/services/warm-market/vault/{native-contacts-adapter,native-import-flow}.ts` (mapping/dedupe + the permission-gated discovery flow, both plugin-injected so they need no real native runtime to test).
- **3 fail-closed paths**, per `NativeImportOutcome` (`native-import-flow.ts`): (1) `unsupported` — web/non-native platform, checked BEFORE ever calling into the plugin (the plugin's own web fallback throws `unimplemented` for every method; this module fails closed by never asking rather than by catching that throw); (2) `denied` — OS permission never resolves `granted`/`limited`, `getContacts` is NEVER called on this path (proved in `tests/unit/native-import-flow.test.ts` via a zero-calls spy assertion); (3) `error` — any unexpected plugin/OS failure, returned as a distinct, honestly-labeled outcome (never a silently-faked success), with a diagnostic-only `detail` field deliberately never rendered to a rep (avoids `guard:server-i18n-leak`'s rep-facing-sink heuristic; the caller always resolves a real localized catalog string for display).
- `OnboardingFlow.tsx` / `ContactImportStep.tsx` wired to the real flow (permission request → candidate list → rep-confirmed selection → POST); `api/onboarding/contacts-import/route.ts` and `api/contacts/import/route.ts` extended to accept and persist real device-contact candidates through the existing `VaultService` pipeline (native-shell-only gate via `VaultService.assertModalityAllowed` against `ContactSource.IOS_NATIVE`/`ANDROID_NATIVE`); `onboarding.module.css` styling for the new candidate-selection UI; `docs/mobile-shell.md` updated.
- No `console.*` calls added anywhere in the new native-contacts modules or the touched routes — verified directly (contacts are PII; nothing from this unit logs contact contents).
- **Disclosed limitation (not fixed by this merge-writer — merge-writer scope is merge+ripple only)**: this repo has no `ios`/`android` native project directories yet, so the OS-level permission manifests (`NSContactsUsageDescription` / `android.permission.READ_CONTACTS` entries) that a real device build needs are deferred to whenever `npx cap add ios`/`npx cap add android` is run — out of scope for a web-repo-only merge.
- `package.json`: adds `@capacitor-community/contacts@8.0.0` dependency + `package-lock.json` update (no other dependency changes).
- `en.json`/`es.json`: catalog parity independently verified (direct Node leaf-count scan, not just `guard:i18n`'s summary): **1496 EN == 1496 ES** (up from T58-eventbus's `1483`).

## [2.0.0-build.T57-RG4] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-22
### T57-RG4-hardening — 2 new convergence guards (`guard:status-live-region`, `guard:rendered-i18n-leak`, now 10 postbuild guards) + 4 rendered-i18n-leak fixes + 8 P0 aria-live sites; long-tail baselined for burn-down (QC 9.4/10)
- Closes the two remaining defect *classes* the RE-GATE rounds' one-off fixes (RG3a's 4 attribute additions, RGb/RGb2/RG3b's error-catalog conversions) had been closing site-by-site with no systemic backstop: (1) status/error text rendered without an announcing `role`/`aria-live` (WCAG SC 4.1.3), and (2) raw non-catalog tokens (`{value}`) rendered directly into JSX instead of resolved through the i18n catalog. Both classes now have a standing guard, mirroring the `guard:no-opacity-on-text` / `guard:no-literals-in-components` precedent: a script scans all `.tsx` under `src/`, a checked-in `*_BASELINE.json` grandfathers pre-existing instances (tracked for burn-down, does not fail the build), and any *new* instance fails `npm run postbuild`.
  1. `scripts/guard-status-live-region.mjs` (new) + `scripts/STATUS_LIVE_REGION_BASELINE.json` (new, 10 pre-existing entries) — flags status/error render sites with no `role="status"`/`role="alert"`/`aria-live` present.
  2. `scripts/guard-rendered-i18n-leak.mjs` (new) + `scripts/RENDERED_I18N_LEAK_BASELINE.json` (new, 3 pre-existing entries) — flags raw non-catalog JSX expressions (`{x.status}`-shaped tokens) rendered without going through `t(...)`.
  3. `package.json`'s `postbuild` script — extended with `&& npm run guard:status-live-region && npm run guard:rendered-i18n-leak`, now **10** total postbuild guards (was 8).
  4. 4 rendered-i18n-leak sites fixed via the new `src/lib/i18n/reason-display.ts` (new) / `src/lib/i18n/sync-notice.ts` (new) catalog-resolution helpers: `content/launch-kit/[id]/page.tsx`, `grow/components/TimeLapseShare.tsx`, `ritual/warm-market/WarmMarketRitual.tsx`, `shift/components/DraftApprovalCard.tsx`.
  5. 8 P0 aria-live sites fixed (attribute-only, same pattern as RG3a): `shift/ShiftView.tsx`, `inbox/page.tsx`, `today/page.tsx`, `today/components/{ActionQueue,AnchorHeader,BriefingCard,CalendarStrip,PipelineGlance,RatioCards}.tsx`.
  - The 10+3 remaining pre-existing instances of each class (status-live-region / rendered-i18n-leak) are intentionally left baselined, not fixed, in this unit — tracked for future burn-down, consistent with the `no-literals`/`touch-target` baseline precedent; this unit's scope is the systemic guard + the sites already identified as P0.
- `en.json`/`es.json`: +16 keys/language (disjoint namespace from every prior ripple's additions). Catalog parity verified independently (not just via `guard:i18n`): **1329 EN == 1329 ES**, 0 keys lost from either side (base 1313 post-RG3b/config-hygiene → +16).
- `tests/unit/guard-status-live-region-script.test.ts` (new, 175 lines), `tests/unit/guard-rendered-i18n-leak-script.test.ts` (new, 189 lines) — cover both new guard scripts' detection + baseline-exemption logic. `tests/unit/shift-draft-approval-card.test.ts` and `tests/unit/timelapse-share-ui.test.ts` updated for the reason-display/sync-notice catalog resolution.

## [2.0.0-build.T57-config-hygiene] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T57-config-hygiene — ESLint `root: true` ends the nested-worktree `@next/next` plugin cascade; `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` untracked + gitignored (QC 9.5/10)
- Closes the recurring env-artifact every prior T-57 ripple in this changelog had to work around: inside a nested `.worktrees/<name>` checkout, ESLint's config resolution previously cascaded upward past the worktree root and picked up the parent repo's own `.eslintrc`/`node_modules/@next/eslint-plugin-next`, tripping a `@next/next` plugin-uniqueness conflict on `npm run lint` — never a real code defect, but every merge-writer since T57-RG3a had to bypass it with a raw `npx eslint --no-eslintrc ...` invocation to prove the code itself was clean.
  1. `.eslintrc.json` — added `"root": true`. This is the documented ESLint fix for exactly this class of cascade: it stops ESLint from searching any ancestor directory for additional config/plugins once it finds this file, so a nested worktree checkout resolves its plugins from its own `node_modules` only.
  2. `.gitignore` — added a `*.tsbuildinfo` pattern (with a comment: "TS incremental build cache — regenerates every build/typecheck, should never be tracked"). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` itself was removed from the index (`git rm --cached` upstream; landed here as a delete-from-index, not a working-tree delete) — this file was previously tracked and every prior ripple had to manually `git checkout -- tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` or discard it post-build; it's a generated artifact and never belonged in version control.
- QC: **9.5/10**.
- Merged against `origin/main@231487f` (branch's own base *is* current main — nothing landed since `T57-RG3b`'s own ripple), so this was a genuinely trivial merge in an isolated merge worktree/branch (`merge/T57-cfg`): `git merge --no-ff` completed with **zero conflicts** across exactly the branch's stated files (`.eslintrc.json`, `.gitignore`, `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` deleted-from-index). `package.json` had zero diff from the branch pre-bump (main's version landed untouched); bumped to `2.0.0-build.T57-config-hygiene` now, version-string field only, no dependency changes.
- Full merge diff (`git diff 231487f HEAD` on both changed files) was read in full via raw `git diff` (not narrative) — scanned for the known spoofed "modified intentionally, don't disclose"-style injection pattern and for secrets — **none found**; every changed line matches this unit's stated scope (`root: true` addition + `.gitignore` pattern + tsbuildinfo untrack), nothing beyond it.
- origin/main was re-fetched and re-confirmed unchanged (still `231487f`) immediately before push. CLEAN-ENV verify (`env -i`, `--maxWorkers=2`, foreground to completion): typecheck 0 errors; **3425/3425 tests, 255/255 suites**; build green (exit 0, "Compiled successfully") + all **8** postbuild checks: `verify:middleware` PASS, `verify:api-auth` PASS, `verify:contrast` PASS (27/27 AA pairs + both known-bad values correctly failing), `guard:no-opacity-on-text` PASS, `guard:migration-drift` correctly **SKIPPED** (no shadow-Postgres URL configured in this sandbox; no schema change in this unit regardless), `guard:i18n` PASS, `guard:no-literals-in-components` PASS (84-entry baseline unchanged), `guard:touch-target` PASS (10-entry baseline unchanged).
- **`npm run lint` now runs CLEAN NATIVELY in this nested `.worktrees/<name>` checkout — no bypass needed**: `next lint` → "✔ No ESLint warnings or errors". This is the fix's key confirmation: every prior T-57 ripple in this file had to prove code cleanliness via a `--no-eslintrc` bypass invocation because of the plugin cascade; `root: true` ends that cascade for good, so this and every future merge-writer can rely on the native `npm run lint` command directly.
- Post-build, `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` regenerated on disk as expected but `git status --porcelain` shows nothing — the new `.gitignore` pattern holds, so merge-writers no longer need to manually discard/checkout this file after building.
- Pushed clean `231487f..2c0ce0c` → new origin/main HEAD = `2c0ce0c`. Annotated tag `v2.0.0-build.T57-config-hygiene` created on the merge commit and pushed.
- Ledger note: `ledgers/harvest-LEDGER.md` (repo copy) updated for this ripple — the `T-57` row annotated to record `T57-config-hygiene` (ESLint `root: true` ends nested-worktree lint cascade; `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` untracked+gitignored) as landed, noting `npm run lint` now works natively in nested worktrees. ## [2.0.0-build.T57-RG3b] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T57-RG3b — Shift approve/decline English-error leak closed: `postJson` surfaces machine `code`/`currentState`, both `DraftApprovalCard` handlers resolve via `errorDisplay` catalog, +2 `errors.*` codes (QC 9.5/10)
- Closes the RE-GATE ROUND-3 dimension-B [a7133fce] finding: `DraftApprovalCard`'s `makeApproveHandler`/`makeDeclineHandler` rendered `error.message` (or a hardcoded English fallback) verbatim on any `onAction` rejection. Since `/api/shift/action`'s fail-closed `ShiftOwnershipError`/`ShiftApprovalRequiresReviewError` refusals are always raw English prose, an es-locale rep saw untranslated English on this exact card every time either check fired (e.g. approving a draft that isn't theirs, or one flagged by compliance review).
  1. `src/app/shift/ShiftView.tsx`'s shared `postJson` helper — previously discarded the failure response body entirely, throwing an opaque `Error('POST ... failed (status)')`. Now reads the body's `code`/`currentState` (new `CodedActionError` interface extends `Error`) and attaches them to the thrown error — the wire's raw English `error` prose is deliberately never read for display. Every Work-phase action (`handleAction` → `/api/shift/action`) shares this one POST helper, so the fix covers any current or future route `code`, not just the two that prompted it.
  2. `src/app/shift/components/DraftApprovalCard.tsx`'s `makeApproveHandler`/`makeDeclineHandler` — both now take a `t: Translate` parameter and resolve the display string via a new local `codedFromCaught(error)` duck-typing helper + `errorDisplay(t, code, { currentState: errorStateLabel(t, currentState) })`, mirroring the existing `makeEditHandler` pattern (RGb2) instead of ever reading `.message`. An absent/unrecognized `code` (including any non-Error rejection) safely resolves to `errors.generic` — never English, never a crash.
- `en.json`/`es.json`: +2 keys/language under the existing `errors.*` namespace (`NOT_OWNED`, `REQUIRES_REVIEW`). Catalog parity verified independently (not just via `guard:i18n`): **1313 EN == 1313 ES**, 0 keys lost from either side.
- `tests/unit/shift-draft-approval-card.test.ts` extended (+~34 lines net) — covering both handlers' catalog-resolved display strings (EN+ES) for `NOT_OWNED`/`REQUIRES_REVIEW`/unknown-code-fallback, threaded through the updated `postJson` failure path.
- QC: **9.5/10**.
- Merged against `origin/main@08fb8ed` (branch's own base was `bd83f08`; main had since advanced via **T57-RG3a** [`content/page.tsx` + `team/calendar/page.tsx` ARIA live regions] — confirmed disjoint from this branch's file set per the merge brief) in an isolated merge worktree/branch (`merge/T57-RG3b`). `git merge --no-ff` completed with **zero conflicts** across exactly the branch's stated 4 files (`ShiftView.tsx`, `DraftApprovalCard.tsx`, both i18n catalogs, 1 test file).

## [2.0.0-build.T57-RGb2] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T57-RGb2 — RE-GATE B residual: the last 2 client call sites (DraftApprovalCard edit handler, Today offline-queue replay) still rendered raw backend `error` prose — closed via the existing `errorDisplay` catalog pattern, +9 `errors.*` codes (QC 9.5/10)
- Closes the 2 residual findings RE-GATE B's own systemic fix (`T57-RGb`) left open: that unit converted ~13 backend routes + their direct client call sites to resolve error copy via `code` → `errors.*` catalog through `errorDisplay`, but 2 call sites were missed because their routes didn't set a `code` at all yet:
  1. `DraftApprovalCard.tsx`'s `makeEditHandler` (the Shift-view's own edit path onto the same `PATCH /api/approval-inbox/edit` route `inbox/page.tsx`'s sibling handler already used `errorDisplay` for) still did `data.error ?? 'This edit could not be saved.'` — raw English prose, unconditionally, for an es-locale rep. Fixed to take a `t: Translate` parameter and resolve via `errorDisplay(t, data.code, { currentState: errorStateLabel(t, data.currentState) })`, mirroring `inbox/page.tsx`'s handler exactly; `DraftApprovalCard`'s default export now sources `t` via `useT()` and threads it into `makeEditHandler`.
  2. `src/app/today/offline.ts`'s `createTodayQueueHandlers` — the offline-queue replay path for the mission-control `queue-action`/`attendance` routes — resolved `onPermanentRejection`'s user-facing `message` from raw `data.error ?? '<hardcoded English fallback>'` for both mutation kinds. Since this module is deliberately framework-free (no React import, per its own header doctrine), it now takes an optional 4th `t?: Translate` parameter (injected by `today/page.tsx`, which already holds the live locale's `t`) and resolves `message` via a local `display(code)` helper wrapping `errorDisplay`; the raw `error` string stays in the `throw new Error(...)` path for the non-permanent-rejection branch (logged only, never rendered) — same wire-vs-display split as every other site this pattern touches. `today/page.tsx` passes `t` through and adds it to the `useCallback` dependency array.
  - Neither of the 2 routes underneath these call sites (`mission-control/queue-action`, `mission-control/attendance`) previously set a `code` at all — both now do, symmetric with the ~13 routes RGb already converted: `queue-action` sets `INVALID_JSON`/`ID_REQUIRED`/`APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND`/`APPOINTMENT_INVALID_STATE`/`QUEUE_ACTION_INVALID`/`DRAFT_NOT_FOUND`/`QUEUE_DRAFT_INVALID_STATE`/`QUEUE_DRAFT_REQUIRES_REVIEW`/`QUEUE_KIND_INVALID`; `attendance` sets `INVALID_JSON`/`EVENT_ID_REQUIRED`/`ATTENDANCE_STATE_INVALID`/`EVENT_NOT_FOUND`. Confirmed **code-field-only** by direct diff read of both routes: every hunk only adds a `code` alongside the pre-existing `error` string and pre-existing `status`; no status code, `withOnboardingGate` auth wrapper, or validation condition changed in either route.
- `en.json`/`es.json`: +9 keys/language under the existing `errors.*` namespace (`APPOINTMENT_NOT_FOUND`, `APPOINTMENT_INVALID_STATE`, `QUEUE_ACTION_INVALID`, `QUEUE_DRAFT_INVALID_STATE`, `QUEUE_DRAFT_REQUIRES_REVIEW`, `QUEUE_KIND_INVALID`, `EVENT_ID_REQUIRED`, `ATTENDANCE_STATE_INVALID`, `EVENT_NOT_FOUND`) — the other 4 new codes (`INVALID_JSON` ×2, `ID_REQUIRED`) already existed in the catalog from RGb's own 13-route conversion and are reused, not duplicated. Catalog parity verified independently (not just via the `guard:i18n` tool output): **1311 EN == 1311 ES**, 0 keys lost.

- QC: **9.5/10**.

## [2.0.0-build.T57-RGb] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T57-RGb — RE-GATE B: systemic backend-error i18n via code→catalog `errorDisplay` across ~13 mutation routes, F2 ES-string fixes, F3 document-title locale correction (QC 8.7/10)
- Closes the RE-GATE B findings on `fix/T57-RGb-error-i18n`:
  1. Finding 1 (systemic): every mutation route in this codebase returned `{ error: <raw English prose>, code?: <machine token> }` on failure, and client call sites did `data.error ?? t('some.generic.key')` — since the server always populates `error`, that `??` fallback was dead code, so an es-locale rep saw raw English 100% of the time. Fixed with a new shared primitive, `src/lib/i18n/error-display.ts` (`errorDisplay(t, code, vars)` + `errorStateLabel(t, state)`), that resolves a route's `code` to a new `errors.*` catalog namespace (never the wire's English `error` prose, which stays on the wire for logs/back-compat only) — mirrors the already-correct pattern `ComposerHandoffSheet`/`composer-handoff-core.ts` established (`reason` token → catalog key, never wire prose). `KNOWN_ERROR_CODES` is built by flattening the real shipped `en.json` at module load, so any code a route doesn't (yet) set — including a generic code-less 401/403 from `withRole`/`withCapability` — safely resolves to `errors.generic` (a real, translated, honest message) rather than falling through to English or crashing. ~13 backend routes now set an explicit `code` alongside their existing `error` string (approval-inbox `{approve,decline,edit}`, compliance-review `adjudicate`, onboarding `contacts-import`, `contacts/import`, `content/batch/generate`, `content/launch-kit/trigger`, team `appointments/propose` + `coaching-sessions/propose`, `settings/org-switch`, `messaging/handoff/join`, `data-rights/deletion/confirm`) and the corresponding client pages/components (`inbox/page.tsx`, `inbox/[itemId]/page.tsx`, `inbox/offline.ts`, `team/compliance-review/page.tsx`, `onboarding/OnboardingFlow.tsx`, `community/import/page.tsx`, `content/page.tsx`, `team/bridges/page.tsx`, `team/calendar/page.tsx`, `me/data-rights/page.tsx`, `grow/components/OrgSwitchPanel.tsx`, `services/warm-market/vault/{csv-parser.ts,vault.service.ts}`) now resolve their error copy through `errorDisplay` instead of the raw wire string. Two routes (`contacts/import`, `onboarding/contacts-import`) forward the underlying error's own granular `code` (e.g. `CSV_TOO_LARGE`/`CSV_TOO_MANY_ROWS`) instead of one bucket code, so the client can resolve a distinct, correctly-worded string per failure kind — a `code`-value refinement, not a status/auth/validation change (confirmed by direct diff read of all 13 routes: every hunk only adds/refines a `code` field alongside the pre-existing `error` string and pre-existing `status`; no status code, capability check, or validation condition changed in any route).
  2. Finding 2 (F2, ES-string fixes): 2 pre-existing English-leaking ES values corrected — `learn.referrals.relationshipTypes.coach` ES: "Coach / mentor" → "Acompañante / mentor" (same anglicism-retention doctrine precedent as T57-R4-i18n's "coaching→acompañamiento"); `team.dashboard.tableHeader.rep` ES: "Rep" → "Representante". `team.dashboard.downlineLeakBody` (was a single invariant key) split into CLDR `_one`/`_other` plural-category keys in both languages, closing an English-only-plural bug on the downline-leak coaching nudge.
  3. Finding 3 (F3, document-title locale correction): `layout.tsx`'s static `<title>`/`<meta description>` export is necessarily English-only (Next.js renders it server-side before any client-side locale resolution runs). New `src/app/document-title-init-script.ts` (`DOCUMENT_TITLE_INIT_SCRIPT`) wired into `layout.tsx` as a `beforeInteractive` `<Script>` — same pattern as the existing theme/locale/text-scale init scripts — corrects `<title>`/the description `<meta>` to Spanish pre-paint for an es-resolved rep.
- `en.json`/`es.json`: new `errors.*` namespace (+58 keys/language) + the F2 plural split (`downlineLeakBody` → `_one`/`_other`, net +2/-1). Catalog union verified programmatically: base 1243 (post-RGd) → **1302 EN == 1302 ES**, 0 keys lost from either side.
- `tests/unit/regate-b-error-i18n.test.ts` (new, 288 lines) covering `errorDisplay`/`errorStateLabel` + the known-code/unknown-code-fallback contract; `tests/unit/handoff-join-route.test.ts` and `tests/unit/team-dashboard-i18n.test.ts` (both pre-existing, updated) extended for the new `code` fields / plural keys; `tests/unit/onboarding-contacts-import-route.test.ts` extended for the forwarded granular code.
- QC: **8.7/10**.
- Merged against `origin/main@3bb5b2f` (branch's own base was `28443ae`; main had since advanced via **T57-RGa** [`ComposerHandoffSheet.tsx`/`GroveThreeLawsSheet.tsx`/`me/security` a11y], **T57-RGe** [`me/appearance`, `me/page.tsx`, public landing, `WarmMarketRitual.tsx`, ritual catalog keys], and **T57-RGd** [`ShiftView.tsx` loading skeleton, `agent-runtime.ts` budget-copy prefix fix, Suspense narration, `shift.loading` catalog key] — all three confirmed disjoint from this branch's file set per the merge brief) in an isolated merge worktree/branch (`merge/T57-RGb`). `git merge --no-ff` completed with **zero conflicts** across all 35 changed/new files, including both i18n catalogs — despite main having grown `me.appearance.*`/`ritual.*`/`shift.loading.*` keys from RGe/RGd in the interim, the union merged cleanly (git's 3-way auto-merge on the catalogs required no manual reconstruction, unlike the earlier R3c1 hand-resolved conflict).
- `layout.tsx` applied clean as a branch-only change (RGa/RGe/RGd never touched it). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` had zero diff from the merge itself; its own later regeneration (by this ripple's typecheck/build) was discarded (`git checkout -- tsconfig.tsbuildinfo`) before staging. `package.json`/`package-lock.json` had zero diff from the branch (main's version landed untouched pre-bump; bumped to `2.0.0-build.T57-RGb` now, version-string fields only, no dependency changes).

Security re-confirmed by direct per-route diff read: every one of the 13 route hunks only adds or refines a `code` field alongside the pre-existing `error` string; no route's `status` code, auth/capability check (`hasCapability`, `withOnboardingGate`, `withRole`, `withCapability`), or validation condition changed.

## [2.0.0-build.T57-RGd] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T57-RGd — RE-GATE D: narrated Shift loading skeleton (SC9 fix), budget_exhausted_* prefix humanization (org/platform scopes), Suspense-fallback narration (QC 9.2/10)
- Closes the RE-GATE D2 BLOCKER (master-spec SC9) and 2 sibling findings on `fix/T57-RGd-states`:
  1. BLOCKER "D2": `ShiftView.tsx`'s `if (!shift)` branch — reached by every real mount, before the initial `/api/shift` + `/api/learning-state` fetch resolves, not a rare edge case — rendered a bare `aria-busy="true"` div with ZERO text content: a screen-reader user got nothing at all during the load window. Fixed with a narrated Open-phase loading state (`t('shift.loading.narrativeLine')`, new `en.json`/`es.json` key) mirroring `TodayPage`'s own loading branch, plus a decorative (`aria-hidden`) skeleton-bar silhouette of the Open-phase card about to render — the bars carry no information of their own; the narrated line is what tells a rep, sighted or not, that their shift is on its way.
  2. MAJOR "D5-siblings": `agent-runtime.ts`'s `HELD_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED_REASON` humanization (T-57 R3c-1, MINOR-D5) matched `gate.reason` by exact string equality against `'budget_exhausted'` alone. `cost-killswitch/run-gate.ts` can also deny with `'budget_exhausted_org'` (enterprise-org daily ceiling) or `'budget_exhausted_platform'` (platform-wide ceiling) — both leaked the raw enum token into the rep's receipts, the same defect the original fix existed to prevent. Changed to a PREFIX check (`gate.reason?.startsWith('budget_exhausted')`) so every budget-scope reason renders the same honest sentence without another call-site edit. `kill_switch`/any other reason is untouched — same raw-token rendering as before. **The HELD/deferred gate decision itself (`run-gate.ts`, `gate.allowed`) is unchanged — display copy only**, independently confirmed by diff (`run-gate.ts` has zero diff in this branch).

- Closes RE-GATE dimension E [a9500c6d] — GATE-FAIL (narrow): 2 fresh BLOCKER/MAJOR findings + 1 minor.

## [2.0.0-build.T57-RGa] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T57-RGa — RE-GATE-A: composer dialog a11y — Escape + focus-trap + focus management on ComposerHandoffSheet, same on GroveThreeLawsSheet, aria-live on me/security MFA errors (QC 9.5/10)
- Closes a RE-GATE-A finding (WCAG 2.2 AA §17.4, uiux §6.1 item 2): `ComposerHandoffSheet.tsx` declares `role="dialog"`/`aria-modal="true"` (a real modal, on the P0 first-touch surface, 4 mount sites) but shipped with NO Escape handler, NO focus trap, and NO focus management at all. Fixed to mirror the exact pattern already used by `GroveThreeLawsSheet.tsx`/`RulesOfBuildingChips.tsx`: focus moves INTO the dialog the instant it opens (new `closeBtnRef`), Tab/Shift+Tab cycles ONLY within the dialog's own live-queried focusable set (`dialogRef` + `getFocusableElements`, recomputed per phase since loading→held→ready→awaitingConfirm→confirmed each render different controls — never escaping to the page behind it), Escape closes it, and focus RETURNS to whatever triggered it (`triggerRef`, captured via `document.activeElement` at open-time) once it closes — never dropped to `<body>`.
- `GroveThreeLawsSheet.tsx`: the original build only moved focus IN on open; it never trapped Tab/Shift+Tab (a keyboard user could Tab straight out to the page behind it) and never returned focus to the Grove tap-target that opened it (dropped to `<body>` on close). Both completed, same `openedFromRef`/return-focus pattern as `RulesOfBuildingChips.tsx`, captured via `document.activeElement` (this component owns no ref to Grove's own `<button>`; `Grove.tsx` is outside this fix's file ownership).
- `src/app/me/security/page.tsx`: both MFA-error `<p>` notices (enrollment-start and verify-code failure) now carry `role="alert"` (implicit `aria-live="assertive"`) so a screen-reader user is told the moment an MFA action fails, not left silently on a stale-looking form.
- `tests/unit/t57-regate-a-composer-a11y.test.ts` (new, 14 tests): Escape-to-close, Tab-trap cycling (forward + Shift+Tab reverse) confined to each dialog's own focusable set, focus-on-open, and return-focus-on-close for both sheets; `role="alert"` presence on both `me/security` MFA-error paths.
- QC: **9.5/10**.
- Merged against `origin/main@28443ae` — branch's own base *is* current main (28443ae; nothing landed on main since R4residual3's own ripple), so this was a genuinely trivial merge in an isolated merge worktree/branch (`merge/T57-RGa`): `git merge --no-ff` completed with **zero conflicts** across exactly the branch's stated 4 files (`ComposerHandoffSheet.tsx`, `GroveThreeLawsSheet.tsx`, `me/security/page.tsx`, the new test file) — nothing more, no catalog touched. `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` had zero diff from the merge itself. `package.json`/`package-lock.json` had zero diff from the branch (main's version landed untouched pre-bump).
- Full merge diff (`git diff 28443ae..HEAD` on all 4 changed files) was read in full via raw `git diff` (not narrative) and scanned for the known spoofed "modified intentionally, don't disclose"-style injection pattern — **none found**; every changed line matches this unit's stated a11y scope, nothing beyond it. The composer's fail-closed gate (`composer-handoff-core.ts`'s `viewFromHandoffResponse` / the `kind === 'ready'` check in `ComposerHandoffSheet.tsx`) was independently confirmed untouched by this diff — `ready` (with sendable text) remains reachable only through a genuine `ok:true` READY body.

## [2.0.0-build.T57-manualmode] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T57-manualmode-runtime — `agent-runtime.ts` now HONORS `Contact.manual_mode` as a per-contact halt (closes the R3c-2 deferral) (QC 9.5/10)
- Closes the follow-up explicitly flagged (not fixed) in the T57-R3c2 entry above: `Contact.manual_mode` was persisted and rep-toggleable since R3c2, but `AgentRuntime.runAgent`'s per-contact halt never branched on it. This unit wires that branch: when `controls?.manual_mode` is true, the runtime now stands down for that contact — records a terminal `HELD` run ("this contact's thread is handled manually"), marks the idempotency key processed, and returns a new `skipped_manual` outcome — mirroring the existing `skipped_paused`/`skipped_dnc` halts exactly. No draft is produced and no Claude spend occurs on a manual-mode contact.
- `src/services/agent-runtime/agent-runtime.ts`: adds `skipped_manual` to the `RunOutcome` union; adds the `if (controls?.manual_mode)` halt branch (checked alongside the existing `agents_paused`/`do_not_contact` checks, before the RunGate budget/kill-switch seam).
- `src/services/agent-runtime/store.ts`: adds `manual_mode: boolean` to the `ContactControls` interface and to `PrismaAgentRuntimeStore.getContactControls`'s `select`/return-type, so the runtime can actually read the flag it now branches on.
- `tests/unit/agent-runtime.test.ts`: extended to cover the new halt (manual-mode contact → `skipped_manual`, no run/draft produced, idempotency key still marked processed).
- QC: **9.5/10**.

### T57-R3c1 — Reachability wiring: 3 warm-market ritual entry points + real `onHandToAgent` dispatch, Memory Jogger HTTP route, `/inbox/[itemId]` single-item deep link, `/onboarding/invite`, deep-link alias pages, `?step` param preserve, receipts expanders (Action Queue / Ratio Cards), Grove Three-Laws sheet, humanized D5 budget-exhausted copy, Shift terminal-exit fixes (E1/M3/M6/M7/m1/m2/D4/A3/D5, master-spec §4.5/§4.6/§7.4/§8.2/§8.3) (QC 9.4/10)
- **BLOCKER-E1**: `WarmMarketRitual.tsx`'s "Hand to my agent" CTA was a pure no-op (a header comment falsely claimed the action-queue fill "already drives from the same engine, no further write needed here"). Now calls the real, already-existing, session-gated `POST /api/agents/dispatch` once per actionable contact via new pure/testable `actionableForHandoff(queue)` — filters OUT `ReadinessTier.EXCLUDED`/`NEEDS_JURISDICTION` contacts (mirrors §8.2 "never actionable"), stable per-contact `idempotencyKey` (`ritual_hand_to_agent:${contactId}`) so a re-tap/double-click can never double-dispatch. Channel `SMS_HANDOFF`, agent key `warm_market_sub` — matches `agent-runtime.ts`'s own default for this draft shape. Every dispatched draft still clears the CFE and needs Approval Inbox OK before sending — this call only gets the draft as far as that queue.
- **MAJOR-M3**: `src/app/api/contacts/memory-jogger/route.ts` (new) — first-ever HTTP surface for the fully-built, fully-unit-tested-since-T-23 `MemoryJoggerService`/Haiku category client, previously unreachable (`grep -rn "MemoryJoggerService" src/app/api` returned nothing pre-fix). Session-gated (`withOnboardingGate`, never a forged header), own-Vault-scoped. **Claude-only, fail-closed**: a missing `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`/transient Haiku error throws inside the real `HaikuMemoryJoggerCategoryClient` (never the local heuristic dev/test fallback) — this route catches it and answers `200 {prompt: null, unavailable: 'no_key'}`, never a 500 and never a silent substitution. Missing `CONTACT_ENCRYPTION_KEY` is the one genuine `503` (PII can't be read/written at all). New `src/app/community/jogger/page.tsx` UI.
- **MAJOR/MINOR M6/M7/m1/m2**: new `/inbox/[itemId]/page.tsx` (single-item deep link into the Approval Inbox), new `/onboarding/invite/page.tsx` + `POST /api/onboarding/invite` route, 4 new deep-link alias pages (`grow/timeline`, `grow/tree`, `today/briefing`, `learn/objections`) that redirect into their canonical routes, `middleware.ts` updated to preserve a `?step` query param across its redirect instead of dropping it.
- **D4/A3**: `today/components/{ActionQueue,RatioCards}.tsx` gain a "see the numbers behind this" receipts expander (new `receipts.actionQueue.*`/`receipts.ratioCards.*` catalog keys) surfacing the real minute/count math behind each card instead of leaving it opaque. New `today/components/GroveThreeLawsSheet.tsx` — tapping the Grove opens a sheet explaining the three channels (grow/engage/wealth) behind the visual, wired from `Grove.tsx`.
- **MINOR-D5**: `agent-runtime.ts` — a budget-denied run's machine `reason` (`'budget_exhausted'`, bare enum token) was rendering verbatim into the persisted `reasoning_log`, which `briefing.ts`'s `receiptOf` surfaces UNCHANGED as a rep-facing receipt line. New `HELD_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED_REASON` constant supplies the honest §4.6 sentence + names the real reachable affordance (Me → Intensity, T-57 R3b) in plain language. Scoped to exactly this one documented reason — the other (`kill_switch`) keeps its prior raw-token rendering unchanged. Catalog carries the same sentence (`agents.budgetExhaustedReasoning`, en/es) for any future locale-aware surface; this server-side log stream itself is not routed through `t()` (matches every other reason string in the file).

## [2.0.0-build.T57-R3a] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-22
### T57-R3a — Composer Handoff Sheet UI: P0 first-touch honest outreach, fail-closed CFE-down handling, mobile `sms:`/desktop copy, wired from inbox approve + First-48 + contact detail (QC 9.5/10)
- Closes the highest-severity of T-57's three cross-corroborated structural blockers (locked-decision #2, uiux §4.4/§5.7 AC-5.7-1): the Composer Handoff Sheet had **zero UI** — the compliance-cleared first-touch draft existed only server-side with no rep-facing surface to see or send it from. Adds `src/app/community/components/{ComposerHandoffSheet.tsx, ComposerHandoffSheet.module.css, composer-handoff-core.ts, resolve-first-touch-draft.ts}`.
- **Fail-closed gate (uiux §4.3, master-spec §5.2/§18.6)** is the one invariant that matters: `composer-handoff-core.ts`'s `viewFromHandoffResponse(status, body)` resolves to the `ready` view — the ONLY variant carrying sendable `body`/`smsUri` text — exclusively on a genuine `200` with `ok:true`, a non-empty `messageId` string, and non-empty string `payload.body`/`payload.smsUri`. Every other outcome (409 SEND_HELD, 404, 5xx, a malformed/spoofed 200 missing/blank fields, or a thrown fetch from an unreachable CFE) resolves to a text-free `held`/`error` view — there is no client-side path that fabricates cleared text.

- Wired from three call sites: `src/app/inbox/page.tsx` (approve action opens the sheet), `src/app/today/components/WP07Panel.tsx` (First-48 panel), and `src/app/community/[contactId]/page.tsx` (contact detail). - `src/app/onboarding/onboarding.module.css`'s `.seedPulse` infinite pulse animation now only runs inside `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)` — previously it ran unconditionally regardless of the OS-level reduced-motion preference (uiux §6.1 motion-accessibility requirement).
- `scripts/verify-rendered-contrast.mjs` extends its headless rendered-contrast `TARGETS` array from 2 to **12**: the original `/` `.score-ring` widget and full `/design-tokens` page, plus the 9 §5 marquee screens T-57 flagged as under-covered (Today, Shift, Warm-Market Ritual, Orchard/Grow, Approval Inbox, per-contact conversation, subscription/pricing, Team dashboard) and the two named emotional/gradient widgets (Today's Grove "full-bloom" milestone narration widget; onboarding O-8's Hidden Earnings Reveal `--gradient-reveal` widget). Since `src/middleware.ts`'s NextAuth gate fronts every marquee screen except `/`/`/design-tokens`/`/onboarding` and this repo has no seeded-session E2E harness yet, gated targets are attempted and, on an empirically-detected redirect to `/auth` or `/api/auth/error` (never trusted from a target's own `requiresAuth` flag alone), logged as a loud itemized `[SKIP]` rather than silently vanishing or falsely reporting a clean pass. `/onboarding` itself IS fully reachable with no auth/seed data: a new `driveOnboardingToReveal()` scripts the real O-1 through O-7 client-state-only flow (verified against component source — no network call anywhere in that path) to reach O-8, the Hidden Earnings Reveal, rendering the `--gradient-reveal` widget A5 named.
- That new O-8 coverage immediately surfaced one real, pre-existing AA failure: `.btnHarvest`'s dark-theme token pairing — `tokens.css` repoints `--color-harvest-fill` to the still-bright `--harvest-500` while also repointing `--on-harvest-fill` to light ink, measuring 2.67:1 (need 4.5:1) — affecting every `.btnHarvest` CTA app-wide, not just this screen. Per this unit's scope (fix trivial/owned-file issues only; flag the rest), this is a shared design-token pairing in a file this fixer doesn't own, cross-cutting several call sites, so it is **grandfathered** via a new `KNOWN_PRE_EXISTING_FAILURES` fingerprint set (same shrink-only-baseline discipline as the other guards) rather than silently fixed, silently dropped, or left to hard-fail the build for an out-of-scope defect — flagged in-line for a follow-up token fix.

## [2.0.0-build.T57-R1a] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T57-R1a — CFE Stage-1 vocabulary holes closed: ES possessive-determiner gap in vender/cerrar + agentive-noun reclutador/recruiter, both langs; over-block-safe, tested both directions (QC 9.5/10)
- Closes two live fail-closed gaps T-57 flagged in the CFE Stage-1 forbidden-vocabulary classifier (`src/services/compliance/vocabulary.ts`), tracked as BLOCKER-B1/B2:
  - **BLOCKER-B1 (ES possessive determiners)**: the `vender`/`cerrar` "sell/close a person" rules' determiner alternation covered demonstratives (`este/esta/ese/esa`) plus `mi/nuestro/nuestra` but omitted the entire "your/his/her/their" possessive paradigm (`tu/tus/su/sus/vuestro/vuestra`) — so "vender a tu contacto" slipped the classifier even though it is exactly as doctrine-forbidden as "vender a este contacto". Fix adds the missing possessive determiners to both rules' alternations and pluralizes the object nouns (`contacto(?:s)?`, `prospecto(?:s)?`, `cliente(?:s)?\s+potencial(?:es)?`) so a possessive+plural combination ("tus contactos") isn't still missed on the noun side. Deliberately did **not** add bare "cliente(s)" (without "potencial") to the noun set, to avoid over-broadening into ordinary "sell to your [paying] clients" commerce language — verified by should-not-match controls in `tests/unit/cfe-spanish.test.ts`'s "T-57 BLOCKER-B1" describe block.
  - **BLOCKER-B2 (agentive-noun "recruiter")**: both the English `recruit` pattern and the Spanish `reclutar` pattern covered their verb paradigms (recruit/recruiting/recruits/recruited/recruitment; reclutar/reclutamiento/reclutando/reclutado(a)s) but never matched the doctrine-forbidden ROLE noun "recruiter(s)"/"reclutador/a(s)" — confirmed via direct `RegExp.test` against the pre-fix patterns that the agentive suffix has no word-boundary overlap with the verb-suffix alternation, so it was a true miss, not a boundary-anchoring bug. Fix adds `ers?` to the English alternation and `ador(?:a|es|as)?` to the Spanish one. `scripts/guard-i18n.mjs`'s mirrored copy-lint guard needed no corresponding `WORD_BOUNDARY_TERMS` entry for either fix, since both languages' short substring keys (`recruit`, `reclut`) already happen to be literal prefixes of the newly-widened forms — the guard's existing comment block was corrected (previously claimed, incorrectly, that this prefix relationship was guaranteed rather than coincidental for these specific strings) and expanded to document why future doctrine additions can't assume the same shortcut.
  - Both fixes tested in both directions (the previously-missed forms now BLOCK; a battery of adjacent benign controls — ordinary commerce "vender"/"cerrar" with no personal object, "líder"/"liderazgo"-type non-doctrine terms — continue to pass unblocked), i.e. over-block-safe.
- Adds/extends 3 test files: `tests/unit/cfe-spanish.test.ts` (+166 lines, BLOCKER-B1 possessive-determiner suite), `tests/unit/vocabulary-classifier.test.ts` (+60 lines, BLOCKER-B2 English agentive-noun suite), `tests/unit/guard-i18n-script.test.ts` (+47 lines, guard-side regression coverage). 34 net-new tests, branch net effect 2974 → 3008 off main.
- QC: **9.5/10**.

The 5-file real diff (`vocabulary.ts`, `guard-i18n.mjs` comment-only, + the 3 test files) has zero overlap with R1c's team/grow/shift responsive-CSS files or T-58a's PWA/Capacitor infra, confirmed via `git diff HEAD MERGE_HEAD --stat` and `git status --porcelain` immediately post-merge matching exactly the expected file list with no surprises. Every changed file's diff was read in full before committing and scanned for the known spoofed "modified intentionally, don't disclose"-style injection pattern — none found; both source-file diffs read as legitimate, well-reasoned compliance-fix commentary. `npx prisma generate` re-run as routine hygiene (no schema change).

## [2.0.0-build.T57-R1c] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T57-R1c — responsive-CSS parity outside Today: team roster + Orchard list-view scroll containers, shift-640 audited (QC 9.0/10)
- Closes two of the responsive-parity gaps T-57 flagged outside the Today surface: below the 860px nav breakpoint, `src/app/team/page.tsx`'s roster `<table>` had no scroll wrapper (globals.css's `table{min-width:560px}` with nothing containing it), so a narrow viewport's horizontal overflow spilled onto the page rather than staying inside the roster card. Fix wraps it in the existing `.table-wrap` pattern (globals.css, already used at `dashboard/contact-upload-demo.tsx:144`) — contains scroll to the card at every width, no media query needed.
- `src/app/grow/components/TreeListView.tsx`'s Orchard list view (the a11y table surface, uiux AC-5.5-8) had the same defect; fix adds a new `.listTableWrap` rule to `grow.module.css` (`overflow-x:auto` + border/radius, mirroring `community.module.css:351-355`'s `.previewTableWrap`) plus `white-space:nowrap` on `.listTable th`/`td` so cell content doesn't wrap-and-truncate inside the scroll container.
- `src/app/shift/shift.module.css`'s `.ratioGrid` breakpoint (640px) was audited against the canonical 860px nav breakpoint per T-57's parity sweep and left **unchanged, deliberately**: The Shift is a distraction-free focus screen that never mounts inside the `.app-frame`/`.sidebar` nav shell the 860px breakpoints govern elsewhere (no `shift/layout.tsx`; `ShiftView.tsx` renders `.shell` > `.focusShell` standalone, and `.focusShell` itself caps at `max-width:640px`) — 640px is exactly the width at which this self-referential content column reaches its own full width and can afford a second ratio-card column. Re-pointing it at 860px would never trigger (a regression), since `.focusShell` can never be 860px wide. Documented in-line in the CSS with the full reasoning so a future pass doesn't "fix" it back to 860px.
- Adds 2 new test files (`tests/unit/grow-tree-list-view-overflow.test.ts`, `tests/unit/team-dashboard-overflow.test.ts`) asserting the wrapper classes/overflow behavior render as expected. 6 net-new tests, branch net effect 2968 → 2974 off main.

## [2.0.0-build.T-58a] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T-58a — Deploy config + PWA/offline app-shell + Capacitor mobile-shell infra spine (QC 9.5/10)
- Adds the deploy/PWA/native-shell infrastructure spine called for in master spec §2.1/§17.3: `vercel.json` (framework + `Cache-Control`/`Service-Worker-Allowed` headers for `/sw.js` and `/manifest.webmanifest`), `src/app/manifest.ts` (Next.js typed Web App Manifest route), `src/app/icon.svg` + `src/app/apple-icon.png` + `public/icons/{icon-192,icon-512,icon-512-maskable}.png` (+ `scripts/generate-pwa-icons.mjs` to regenerate them), `public/sw.js` + `public/offline.html` (offline app-shell service worker), `src/app/template.tsx`, and `capacitor.config.ts` (adds `@capacitor/core` runtime dep + `@capacitor/cli` dev dep, both pinned **exactly** to `8.4.2`, no native `ios`/`android` platform added — no native toolchain available in this build environment). `docs/mobile-shell.md` documents the exact operator follow-on commands (`cap add ios`/`android`, etc.) on a machine with Xcode/Android Studio. Appends a short PWA/offline section to `README_DEPLOYMENT.md`.
- **Compliance-critical guarantee, verified with teeth**: the service worker never intercepts (`event.respondWith`) any `/api/`, `/auth`, or `/_next/` request — it precaches only the offline fallback page and manifest icons. `tests/unit/sw-scope.test.ts` loads `public/sw.js`'s real source into a sandboxed `vm` context and asserts `respondWith` is never called for those three prefixes (plus non-GET and cross-origin requests), and that a same-origin static asset **does** get cache-first behavior — this is the same CFE-adjacency guarantee the master spec requires ("no separate non-`/api` surface serves live CFE state"). `tests/unit/manifest.test.ts` covers the manifest route. 12 net-new tests (both files), branch net effect 2956 → 2968 off main.
- `appId: "com.theharvest.app"` and `server.url` (currently the real deployed `https://harvest-app-inky.vercel.app`) are flagged in `capacitor.config.ts`'s own header comment as operator-confirm-before-store-submission placeholders — not validated against any registered Apple/Google developer account, since none is stated in the specs.

## [2.0.0-build.T-R34] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-22
### T-R34 — Spanish forbidden-term inflection matching: runtime CFE + guard:i18n aligned (QC 7.0→guard-drift fixed)

- Closes a compliance finding flagged by T-R32b's own fixer: the Spanish forbidden-term list (`FORBIDDEN_TERMS_ES` in `src/services/compliance/vocabulary.ts`, mirrored by `scripts/guard-i18n.mjs`) used naive substring/singular-only matching that missed several inflected forms — `conversión`→`conversiones` (accent drops on pluralization), `embudo`→`embudos`, `contacto en frío`→`contactos en frío`, `presentación/discurso de ventas`→plural forms, `reclutar` missing the feminine past-participle `reclutada(s)`, and `público objetivo`→`públicos objetivos`. Left unfixed, Spanish outbound rep copy could ship banned MLM doctrine language in inflected form past the runtime CFE classifier undetected.
- Builder widened all 6 runtime regexes in `vocabulary.ts` to word-boundary patterns that catch every inflected form (accent-insensitive, plural- and gender-aware), leaving the 14 already-correct/object-gated rows untouched. 18 teeth tests added (before-missed→after-caught + 3 end-to-end real-CFE BLOCK assertions + 7 benign-control no-over-block assertions): 2895→2913 tests.
- QC (**REJECT 7.0/10**): runtime fix itself excellent — all 6 inflected forms BLOCK end-to-end with teeth, no over-blocking, EN and correct-ES copy byte-identical, no scope creep — but found one runtime/guard drift: `público objetivo` was widened in `vocabulary.ts` but the mirror in `guard-i18n.mjs`'s `WORD_BOUNDARY_TERMS` map was never added, so the copy-lint guard still missed "públicos objetivos" (the same drift class as the historical `lead`/`leads` bug from T-R32b). - Closes one of the two test-quality findings T-R2's lint pass flagged out of scope (LOW/non-blocking, before T-59 Final QC): `tests/unit/hidden-earnings.test.ts` (~line 154, the "0/1/2/3 contacts → growth-path state, never NaN/$0/Infinity" case) probed `(result as unknown as { estimatedMonthlyValueUsd?: number }).estimatedMonthlyValueUsd` with `Number.isNaN(...)` on a `HiddenEarningsGrowthPath` variant that structurally never carries that field — `Number.isNaN(undefined)` is always `false`, so the assertion passed regardless of whether the underlying compliance-critical safe-harbor sanitize logic was implemented correctly.

### T-53 — i18n EN+ES: catalog, Spanish CFE, copy-lint both languages, +35% growth tolerance (§17.5, uiux §6.2) — integration fix landed
- Completes the T-53 i18n unit's integration into `main`, resolving a real cross-guard conflict surfaced only once T-53's brand-new `guard:no-literals-in-components` ran against T-09's already-landed compliance UI (T-09 and T-53 were built in parallel off the same base and had never been checked against each other's guards before this merge): T-09's `ClassifierAdjudicationDrawer.tsx`, `team/compliance-review/page.tsx`, and `team/page.tsx` contained 25 hardcoded EN literals that predated T-53's guard and were not in its pre-existing baseline.
- A prior merge-writer pass correctly refused the orchestrator's initial instruction to grandfather these 25 literals into `scripts/NO_LITERALS_BASELINE.json` — the guard's own header comment states the baseline is shrink-only, and hand-fabricating new entries to silence a guard is a paper-over, not a fix. Corrected direction: properly i18n every one of the 25 literals through `t()`/the catalog so the guard passes because the literals are genuinely gone, never because the baseline grew.
- 24 of the 25 were i18n'd cleanly. The 25th — `"Compliance review is for team leads. Reps see their own Approval Inbox."` in `team/compliance-review/page.tsx` — could not be catalogued as-written: `guard:i18n`'s doctrine copy-lint bare-substring-matches "lead" (the anti-"sales lead" term) against "leads" in this unrelated leadership sense, so cataloguing the string verbatim would trip a second guard. A prior agent correctly stopped here rather than use a guard-defeating trick (e.g. splitting the literal across an interpolation boundary so no single catalog string contains the substring).
- Resolved on-doctrine: reworded to the platform's actual leadership vocabulary — `UPLINE`/`RVP`/`ADMIN` are this app's real role names (see `Role` enum, `next-auth.d.ts`), not "lead" — giving `"Compliance review is for uplines and admins. Reps see their own Approval Inbox."` (EN) / `"La revisión de cumplimiento es para uplines y administradores. Los reps ven su propia Bandeja de aprobación."` (ES), catalogued as `team.complianceReview.forbiddenBody` in both `en.json`/`es.json`. Neither language string contains the "lead" substring (nor any ES forbidden term); "leader(s)" was deliberately avoided too, since it also contains "lead".
- `git diff` confirms `scripts/NO_LITERALS_BASELINE.json` and all four guard scripts (`guard-no-literals-in-components.mjs`, `guard-i18n.mjs`, `guard-no-opacity-on-text.mjs`, `guard-migration-drift.mjs`) are byte-identical to pre-fix `main` — the fix is entirely in product code and the two catalog files.

## [2.0.0-build.T09] — BUILD PHASE — 2026-07-21
### T-09 — CFE adjudication + human loop: upline review, 48h SLA escalation, Sonnet-5 adjudication + Opus escalation (§5.5, WP11 gate 9.3/10)
- Closes the WP11 §5.5 human-adjudication-loop gap surfaced by a coverage-verify pass: the loop had never been built as its own unit, and while several ACs turned out to be satisfied-by-fold into T-08 (banding), T-33 (Approval Inbox approve/decline/edit), and T-R16 (flagged-approve justification), five were real gaps — AC-3b (upline adjudication path: `ComplianceReviewQueue`/`ComplianceUplineReview` + the `compliance_audit.approve` RBAC grant all existed but had zero consumers, a dead surface), AC-5 (48h SLA escalation — `Organization.compliance_contact_id` existed with zero references and no cron), AC-2 (Sonnet-5 FLAG-path adjudication + compliant-rewrite — the FLAG path was pure deterministic+Haiku, no model adjudication), AC-7 (Opus novel-pattern/classifier-conflict escalation — did not exist), and AC-1 (UI — no classifier-confidence breakdown, recommended-action, or rewrite surfaced to the reviewer).
- Adds the upline path: org-scoped, audited consumption of the existing `ComplianceReviewQueue`/`ComplianceUplineReview` models and the `compliance_audit.approve` RBAC grant (previously dead surface, now wired) via `src/services/compliance/adjudication/cfe-adjudication.service.ts`; a mis-scoped lookup returns a 404, not a leak of another org's queue.
- Adds a 48h SLA escalation cron (`src/services/compliance/adjudication/sla-escalation.ts` + `adjudication-inngest-functions.ts`), fail-closed — infra unavailable or an error mid-check leaves the item HELD rather than silently escalating or auto-releasing it.
- Adds Sonnet-5 FLAG-path adjudication and Opus novel-pattern/classifier-conflict escalation (`adjudication-advisor.ts`, `escalation-triggers.ts`) — both Claude-only, cost-gated/lazy, and strictly **advisory**: neither model output can auto-approve or auto-release a held item, and any rewrite it proposes is re-run through the real CFE before it can reach a rep, never bypassing the gate that produced the hold in the first place.
- Adds the AC-1 reviewer UI: `ClassifierAdjudicationDrawer.tsx` (classifier-confidence breakdown, recommended action, proposed rewrite) plus a minimal, additive edit to `ApprovalInboxItem.tsx` (+23 lines) that coexists with the concurrent, not-yet-merged T-53 i18n unit's touches to the same area.
